Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the biodiversity space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 5 and the convergence rate is O(949/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 5 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the biodiversity structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 949. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
